Two States : (Existance | Null)
Existance defined as Not Null: The Set of All Properties

Existance cast shaddow / leaves hole in the Null (Plain)
Has all properties of Existance with - sign; equevelnt to hole in semiconductores
Hole has the inverse of any and all properites of existance but not the properity of existance: it is a shaddow of existance on the Null Plain. Existance requires Two Enities The Existance Enity (as defiend by any arberturally selected properity) and the Shaddow of The Existance Enity  (as defiend by the Conjugate of the arberturally selected properity: Note Mathematical Complex Conjugate)

At One Dimition there is One Phase formed by Existance and Conjugate[Existance]; {Existance | Conjugate[Existance]}; a Vector in a ComplexPlane.

{Existance | Conjugate[Existance]} pair are now addressed per the matholoty from Chapeer 5 and above of The Power Of Alpha by MacGregor. The mathematical implimentaion however will be quantified on a reflection count basis using matirx approch.          

'';;




The {Existance | Conjugate[Existance]} pare will be approched as a Spinor per the work of Cartan in The Theory of Spiniors. The The {Existance | Conjugate[Existance]} pair Plankette form the Priodial 'Partical'





Need both dimention AND Phase to Define structure.

Each dimention has phases
	The phases are quantum in nature
	Each dimention has phase specific phase max count
	Dim NULL = NULL
	Dim 1 has 2
	Dim 2 has 4
	Dim 3 has 8
